# Totem Legend — rules for Claude

The owner (Thai speaker) builds this game across many chats. Reply in Thai. Commit and push finished work to this repo; no zip files.

## Design rules (owner's, apply to every hero and system)
1. **12 Tails style.** Skill trees: Basic + Class A + Class B (two clearly different play styles) + Class C (12 nodes: Basic / A / B columns, unlocked at Lv 55 / 60 / 70 / 80 — squeezed to 51 / 52 / 54 / 56 by the Lv 60 cap, see Stats). Zigzag levelling between a row-1 skill and its partner. A and B must be different play styles (not the same kit with other numbers). Each C node is an upgrade of, or a new skill tied to, the Basic / A / B skill in the same row.
2. **No percent effects.** Use flat values (ATK +X, damage taken −X per hit, shield = level × n). Random chances become counters ("every 4th hit"). Exception (owner 2026-11-19): the item system may keep its percents and chances — special stats, item traits, refine success, drill holes, drop rates (items.js / ITEM_SYSTEM_TH.md). Owner 2026-11-22 (RO stats): runes may use RO-style percents too (race / element damage %, ATK % …); hero skills still may not.
3. **Skill power is written as ATK×n or MATK×n** (TAL was renamed MATK with the RO stats, 2026-11-22). The number shown already includes the hero's coefficient (`CHAR_COEF` in index.html).
4. **Attack speed (ASPD) follows Ragnarok** (owner 2026-11-22, replaces the old "fixed per character" rule): hero base `CHAR_ASPD` at AGI 1 / DEX 1, raised by AGI (÷250) and DEX (÷1000) and by items' ASPD %, then buffs (`aspd()`); cap 190.
5. **Balance (ROLES since 2026-11-24, roles.js + ROLE_SYSTEM_TH.md §11):** every branch has one role (damage / mage / fighter / summoner / debuffer / controller / tank / support) and the bench target is the ROLE's, for the best damage build of the branch: damage 2,450 · mage 2,250 · fighter 2,050 · summoner 1,950 · debuffer 1,800 · controller 1,650 · tank 1,500 · support 1,400 (role factor DEF[role].k × per-branch CAL in roles.js; `tools/roles/role_bench.py hero:A:STR …` sets window.__ROLE_BR). Before roles: target bench score ≈ 2,050 at the Lv 60 cap (tank Rhino ≈ 1,880) for the BEST build of every branch (owner 2026-11-22 'แก้บัค 4': each branch is tuned at its best stat build — `tools/qa/build_matrix.py` runs 7 builds per hero / branch — and a build that does not match the branch's power should stay ≤ ~85–90 %); the older notes below quote the Lv 99 era target 2,450 / 2,250 and the INT-preset tuning of 2026-11-22 morning. Two-way heroes and the 4 pure heroes with branch scales are benched per branch (A alone, B alone).
6. **Pictures are the main effects, code VFX supports them.** Placeholder sprites are fine until real art arrives. Art prompts for ChatGPT: cartoon style matching the characters, one prompt per copy box, sent one at a time.
7. **Selling points (owner 2026-11-21).** (1) branch choice — skill points cannot cover the tree, (2) upgrading / customizing skills with gems — and every gem's effect must SHOW on the real skill (its look when used, its button, its tooltip and tree node), (3) diverse stat builds. New systems must deepen or surface one of these; generic power (refine, % stats) stays small. Audit + proposals: GAME_PILLARS_TH.md (owner picked item 2 = skill stars, built 2026-11-21; his angle: fixed ASPD + boss focus → the game's own hook is 're-tune your skills per boss', so every boss should pose one question a gem answers).

## Stats and damage (Ragnarok formulas, owner 2026-11-22 — RO_STATS_TH.md)
- **Level cap 60 (owner 2026-11-22 'ตัดที่60ก่อนตอนเปิดเกม ปรับเลเวลมอนที่มีตอนนี้ให้ตันแค่60ด้วย')** — level_cap.js (window.LVC, LV_CAP), details + tables in LEVEL_CAP_TH.md. Everything was designed on 1–99: WORLD levels (monsters, map bands, warp signs, bosses, item / material / relic levels) are spread over 1–60 by LVC.n (×0.6, maps/level-zones.js squeezes ZONE right after building it); the item system reads grades / ore tiers / zeny / potions / Star Ore through LVC.o (design level of a level) so a monster drops what it dropped before, item lvReq = LVC.nc (grade 1–9 = Lv 7 … 55); character GATES above 60 are squeezed by LVC.g (1–50 kept, 50–99 → 50–60): totem pole tiers Lv 52/54/56/58/60, C-gem tiers 52/54/56/58, CTIER_LV 51/52/54/56 (skill tree complete by 50 and skill holes 10–50 unchanged → same unlock order). Carving goods keep their design bands (SOCK_LV0). The character's per-level rules: RO points (below), hero bonus, 1 skill point / level. EXP keeps its curve shape via LVC.o (~21 kills at Lv 1 → ~98 for the last level). Saves: play levels above 60 come back at 60 (fitStats trims), saved items re-read lvReq from grade / base (items.js lvFix; storage + old gear market too). Test mode = a Lv 60 hero (presetAt scales PRESETS); bench / test-room dummies MON are Lv 38 / 52 / 44 (design 62 / 85 / 72 in MON_LV0). Every hero was re-tuned at Lv 60 (bench target ≈ 2,050, see rule 5 and the 'Lv 60 retune' note). New world content: write its level on the 1–99 design scale and convert with LVC.n, or write the new level directly and say so.
- **Stats: STR AGI VIT INT DEX LUK** (RO). Total = points + hero bonus + items / buffs. Old 12 Tails → RO: ATK→STR · TAL→INT · INT (SP / cast)→DEX · LCK→LUK · CHA removed (resist = VIT / INT, statuses we inflict = DEX vs monster VIT/2, buff length = skill level only) · DEF is no longer a stat (armour DEF + VIT/2 + AGI/5).
- **Points (RO):** 48 at Lv 1, Lv x → x+1 gives floor(x/5)+3 (555 at Lv 60) · raising x → x+1 costs floor((x−1)/10)+2 · max 99 (one stat ≈ 90 at Lv 60, the rest spread). Hero bonus (like RO job bonus) = floor((Lv−1) × rolled base × 0.0085) ≈ +18 total at Lv 60; the creation roll is hero base (`CLASS_BASE`, 6 stats summing 36) +1 ×3 −1 ×3 +1 all, clamp 3–12.
- **Derived (`derive()`):** ATK = STR + (STR/10)² + DEX/5 + LUK/5 (owl = ranged: DEX main) + weapon ATK + refine (+2/3/5/7 per +1 by weapon level) + flat ATK, × ATK % · MATK = INT + (INT/7)² + DEX/5 + LUK/5 + magic weapon MATK (+ refine) · HIT = Lv + DEX + LUK/3 · FLEE = Lv + AGI + LUK/5 · perfect dodge 1 + LUK/10 % · CRIT 1 + LUK×0.3 (target shield LUK/5), crit ×1.4 skips DEF and FLEE · DEF = armour + VIT/2 + AGI/5 · MDEF = items + INT + VIT/5 + DEX/5 + Lv/4 · MaxHP (300 + 14 Lv)(1 + VIT/100) · MaxSP (40 + 3 Lv)(1 + INT/100) · cast × (1 − DEX/150) · no stat changes cooldown or move speed any more (items / skills only).
- **Hit / miss:** hit % = 80 + HIT − target FLEE (5…95). Normal attacks and physical skills (incl. two-way 'x') can MISS; magic, DoT, crits and `o.sure` hits always land. Monsters: perfect dodge first, then their HIT vs our FLEE (−10 % FLEE per attacker after the 2nd); caster orbs are magic (MDEF, no dodge). Physical damage vs DEF, magic vs MDEF (35/65 formula `defAdjust`).
- Skill damage: physical = coefficient × ATK, magic / heal = coefficient × MATK (`D.patk` / `D.mag`). Two-way heroes (wolf, jaguar, owl, hippo, dragon, lion): normal attack = ATK only; **branch A = ATK only, branch B = MATK only** (owner 2026-11-22 'แก้บัค 4', RO style — the old 'x' mix ATK 30% + MATK 70% is gone, MIX stays defined but unused); Basic-branch 'b' = the higher; C nodes follow their column. So an ATK build plays A, a MATK build plays B. **SP rule (sp_rules.js, owner 'แก้บัค 3'):** physical skills (type 'p') cost 40 % less SP (applied once to SKILLS[k].mp after every installer; SP_RULE.base = old costs; ?spold=1 = old). Flat damage bonuses in the heroes' jgDmgMul hooks add at most the hit's own power (×2) — off-stat builds used to get ×10+ from them (jaguar AGI on the MATK branch benched 4,500).
- Monsters (`monStats`): 20 + 3 points a level spread by role into the six stats + DEF / MDEF; HP (536 + 20 Lv + 10 VIT) × 2.2, ATK (Lv + STR) × 1.86 (×2.2 / ×1.86 since the RO stats, same Lv 60 pacing: 20–31 hits to kill with gear, 15–23 to die), HIT = Lv + DEX (+60 % Lv for bosses), FLEE = Lv + AGI.
- Items: weapon ATK / MATK and armour DEF / MDEF are raw RO-scale numbers; STR … LUK bonuses are small (+1 … +2 a piece, `pairPut`); special stats include RO ones (HIT, FLEE, perfect dodge, ASPD %, cast %, ATK % / MATK %, damage vs race % `r_<race>`, vs element % `e_<el>`, element resistance % `x_<el>`). Orbs: STR AGI VIT INT DEX LUK DEF. Old saves convert once (`conv12`, `it.ro`, bag orbs, `ps._ro` = refund all stat points).

## Testing
- Serve the folder (`python3 -m http.server 8765`) and use Playwright.
- Bench: load `tools/balance_bench.js`, run `benchOnce()` — it benches at the **Lv 60 cap** (window.__BENCH_LV, default 60; dummies at their game levels 38 / 52); `__BENCH_LV=99` / bench_branch.py `LV=99` = the old Lv 99 reference (dummies at design levels 62 / 85) that every bench number in the notes before 2026-11-22 was measured at; set PS._pre=<preset name> when you apply a preset by hand (bench_branch.py PRESET= does); set `window.__BENCH_AVOID` ('m' for an ATK build, 'p' for a TAL build) on two-way heroes only. Pure heroes with two same-stat branches (kitsune, bastet, anubis, sheep): bench each branch alone with `window.__BENCH_SKIP=k=>kCol(k)==='B'` (or bCol / uCol / sBranch, and the other way round; `tools/qa/bench_branch.py` does it). The bench clears `fx` but (since 2026-11-22 'แก้บัค') keeps layer-'none' Core / Keeper objects, calls every *Ensure() and restores the hero state objects (ANU AX BA CAT CR CRX DG HIP JGX KI LI OW RH RHX SHP … snapshotted once per page) at every reset — before, gauges carried over between runs (hippo fullness, kitsune tails …: the group run and later presets started warm) and the owl / hippo / cat / wolf cores were gone during the bench. Hero modules still re-add their core in makers (kEnsure / bEnsure / rhEnsure …).

## Art prompts (what to write when the owner asks for an image or effect prompt)
- Look: friendly hand-painted chibi cartoon matching the game, thick clean dark-brown outlines, cel shading with 2–3 flat tones, soft cartoon glow, bright and warm. Not realistic, not painterly smoke, not 3D. Say this in every prompt.
- Always tell the owner to attach the campfire key art (style) and the hero's character sheet (look). Describe the hero's outfit in the prompt too.
- One prompt per copy box, English prompt, Thai explanation around it. No text / logo / watermark in images.
- Skill effects: ONE effect per image, transparent PNG background, empty space on every side. Never sprite sheets on magenta/green (cut-outs break and red/pink parts vanish). State the view: ground pieces = flat wide ellipse seen 3/4 top-down; streaks/slashes = side view pointing right; upright pieces (spirit, aura, dome) = flat base at the bottom.
- Skill icons: 4×3 sheet 1536×1024, rounded square with thick shiny gold frame, chibi hero acting the skill, background colour per branch, "each icon is a perfect square". Save the prompts as <HERO>_ICON_PROMPTS_TH.md with a slot → skill key table.
- Skill tree backgrounds: vertical 2:3, full-body hero in the lower middle, theme of the branch, top 15% kept simple, medium-light colours (icons sit on top). Basic = start of journey, A / B = their play style, C = legendary with the hero's giant spirit behind.
- Fixing a generated image: tell the owner to brush-select only the part to change in ChatGPT, one character at a time; whole-image edits redraw everything.
- Deliver prompts as copy boxes IN THE CHAT (SendUserMessage, a few boxes per message, grouped by ChatGPT chat with what to attach), every box self-contained: style + format + background inside each box, no 'prompt 0' to send first. Never tell the owner to attach a prompt .md to ChatGPT (2026-11-25 08:09: he attached the whole sea prompt doc and ChatGPT drew a mess). The region monster / mini-boss .txt files were written for whole-file use and still work; for boxes use `python3 tools/monster_prompts.py single <region> a-b` / `python3 tools/miniboss_prompts.py single <file> a-b` (print Markdown, write nothing).

## Placing effects in game
- Toon VFX layer (vfx_layer.js, owner 2026-11-19, only the 4 heroes with generated FX pictures: dragon, kitsune, anubis, lion): every painted piece goes through TV.Piece = anticipation pull-in → overshoot pop + squash/stretch + white flash frame → secondary by type (ground: outlined shockwave ring + rim bits + glow; stand: rise + base ring + rising sparkles; streak: afterimages + speed lines + trail; hit: comic spiky burst + bits; aura: orbiting sparkles) → dissolve into outlined particles sampled from the picture's pixels. Particle family comes from the picture's hue or TV.hint(hero,{piece:'water'|'fire'|'electric'|'sand'|'stone'|'shadow'|'gold'|'light'|'nature'}) at the end of each char_<hero>_fx.js. Pictures a hero file draws itself (beams, pillars, falling suns, bolts) call TV.fxAt / TV.once / TV.trailFx. Frame-strip check: /home/claude/work/strip.py hero keys times. Extended 2026-11-19 to every hero that has painted FX pictures (also wolf, cat, hippo, croc, jaguar, owl, rhino): their own piece classes (WImg, KtImg, HImg, CxImg, JxImg, OxImg, RxImg) keep drawing themselves and TV.attachOld() hooks addFx to add a TV.Companion per piece (contact shadow, rings, bursts, picture-coloured particles, dissolve); wolf_fx.js exposes window.wImg for this. Grounding: TV.Contact puts a dark contact shadow + coloured light pool under upright pieces, heavy elements (water/sand/stone/gold) dissolve with gravity onto the floor, overshoot is soft (1.25). Bastet / axolotl / sheep have no pictures yet and are untouched.
- Ground pieces lie flat on the ground layer under the feet; upright pieces stand from the ground (anchor at the bottom); streaks aim along the attack at weapon height; never lay side-view art flat and spin it.
- Debris (shards, drops) fly with gravity, land, fade. Hit sparks sit on the target body, short.
- Files: effects assets/fx/<hero>/*.webp, icons assets/icons/<hero>/<skill key>.webp (128×128), branch art SKILL_BG_ALL in assets_skillbg.js, per-hero effect code char_<hero>_fx.js.

## Working habits
- Check every visual change with Playwright screenshots before saying it is done, and send the picture to the owner.
- Effect lab (2026-11-21): `python3 tools/fxlab.py strip|clip|hits <hero> …` = frame strips with HIT marks, smooth mp4 clips, hit times after each cast (compare the old look with q=vfx3=0: the numbers must not change). It serves the repo itself when nothing listens on 8765. SVG contact sheet: tools/art_preview.html?f=<hero>_art.js.
- Changed balance numbers → re-run the bench.
- Several chats push to this repo: `git pull --rebase` before every push.
- Play link (owner 2026-11-19): the game is mirrored to the PUBLIC repo gotkatha02/grow-a-garden-deluxe (old garden game replaced, still in its git history) so GitHub Pages serves it: https://gotkatha02.github.io/grow-a-garden-deluxe/index.html?char=dragon . After pushing finished work here, copy the tracked files there too (`git ls-files -z | tar --null -T - -cf - | (cd ../grow-a-garden-deluxe && tar -xf -)`, keep .nojekyll, commit, `git -c push.negotiate=true push`).
- Gear slots (owner 2026-11-21): 5 — top (headgear), armor, rhand (weapon), shoes, acc1 (ONE accessory); 'the gem system carries the depth'. Middle Headgear, Garment and the 2nd accessory were cut 2026-11-21 (Lower Headgear + Left Hand 2026-11-19): old pieces in those slots go back to the bag on load (items.js load), SLOTS.garment stays defined with gone:1 so old cloaks still show / sell, GEAR = the kinds that exist. Their stat budget moved onto the rest (armour DEF 5+1.75g, helm 2.5+.72g, boots 2.5+.67g, town-pair weight helm / boots 1, accessory 1.8) so a full set is as strong as before; old crafted / drop-set pieces are restatted once on load (it.s5). Craft / drop set bonuses 2/4/6 → 2/4/5 (both switched OFF later the same day; the totem seals that carried the set instead were removed 2026-11-25 → no set bonus on gear). Checked: old 8-slot save loads into 5 slots (main stats equal ±2 points, the cut pieces' random special stats are gone), equipment / smith windows, bench sheep A 2,476 · B 2,476, kitsune A 2,446 · B 2,436 (within noise). Plan for the rest (owner said NO to inscriptions; totem seals BUILT, boss relics round 3 approved): GEAR_V3_PLAN_TH.md. Fashion costume slots (FEQ) are separate and unchanged.
- Items: EQ holds instance ids ('i123'), base id = ITEMS[id].base. Old stat keys (str/dex/luk…) map through OLD_KEY.
- Totem seals — REMOVED 2026-11-25 (owner 'ตราประจำแดนเอาออกไป … หมายถึงเอาออกทั้งระบบ'): seals.js is deleted, no seal on any piece, no 2-piece pairs / 4-piece powers / equipment-window panel / smith seal tab / craft land picker / icon badge / sl_ buffs; gear has NO set bonus at all now. Old saves: it.seal / it.sealM / it.trait deleted on load, seal crystals trait_<land> → reroll crystals (items.js sealGone: bag + storage; market.js: listings back to the claim box, reservations refunded). History of the design: GEAR_V3_PLAN_TH.md §3 (kept for reading only). The smith tab that held the seals ('trait' key, same picture) is now the special-stat reroll — see the 'Special-stat reroll' note at the end.
- Boss relics (owner 2026-11-21): round 2 'not useful' → round 3 = MVP-card style (things no hero kit can do / kit or build rule changers, one accessory slot); he then had 6 replaced (Ymir, Typhon, Apep, Quetzal, star cloak, Pharaoh crown — no skill points / skill stars / gem slots / cooldown math) and said 'ชุดนี้ผ่าน'. Table in GEAR_V3_PLAN_TH.md §4. BUILT 2026-11-21 (owner picked '1'): relics.js (after seals.js) — DATA = all 30 (key · land · boss map slot D1b/H1/H2 map boss, D2c/E1 MVP, E2 main · names · type act/rule/build · power text), LIVE = coded ones (only fenrir: lv 80, cd by star 90/84/78/72/66, use = fenrirHowl). A relic = accessory instance {relic, star 1–5} made by AFItems.mkInst with craftStats(land,'acc',grade of the boss level) (no random special stats, lvReq = boss level; refine / break / repair / storage work like any accessory). Save af_relic_v1[_play] = shards / kills / codex / test grants, shared by all heroes of the mode (shards are not bag goods and not tradable yet). Boss kill (boss_fenrir.js onBossDead → RELIC.bossKill('fenrir',e)): +1 shard, relic 1/drop (TIER map 300 · mvp 800 · main 1500; pity 200 / 500 / 800 shards = make one). Stars: a duplicate in the bag or shards = pity × .2/.3/.4/.6. R: key R, PC round button #relicw above AUTO (right click = codex), phone #mobRelic above the skill-set button (tap = use, hold = codex); cooldown fixed by star (gameTime based, not AGI). Fenrir's Fang = every non-ultimate cooldown → 0 (isUlt = sk.ult or th contains 'ULTIMATE'; refuses and keeps its cooldown when nothing is on cooldown) + totemGems.refill() for 'stack' charges; VFX = Fenrir's own howl strip (assets/monsters/fenrirBoss_howl.webp frames 3–34) as a spirit behind the hero (sorted layer) + night pool, rings, Gleipnir chain links, star sparkles, hotbar / wheel buttons flash. Codex window #rlw (A.ui.ttWin): 30 tiles by land (owned gold + ★, live = gold shard ring, 🔒 coming), detail = power by star, drop odds, shards, craft / star-up / equip, 🧪 buttons in test mode; card '🗿 วัตถุโบราณ' under the accessory slot opens it. Test mode puts a ★1 fang on every hero once (RS.tgH). window.RELIC (bossKill, give, open, owned, cdLeft …). Tests in /home/claude/work/relic: func.py <hero> (reset except ultimates + VFX shots), win.py [w h tag] (codex flow), kill.py (real Fenrir kill + forced drop + reload), mobtap.py, play.py. Not done: relic skills as skill gems (the fang is a pure reset — waits for the first damaging relic), shards in the market, dismantling copies past ★5, the other 29 (when their bosses are in maps). - Mobile UI (mobile.js, owner 2026-11-19): RoV-style — floating joystick (left 45%), hold-to-attack button, 🎯 target cycle (boss first), skill wheel 7 slots (inner 3 + outer 4) × 5 sets, tap = auto-target cast, drag = aim (arrow / ground circle), drop on ✕ = cancel; ✎ = RoV-style placement (tray of learned skills on the left: drag skill → slot, slot → slot swaps, slot → tray empties, or tap skill then slot; saved per hero in af_mobsk_<hero>), long-press = skill info; settings button size S/M/L + opacity. Aim indicator shows each skill's real hit area from skill_shapes.js (cone / line / circle around hero / ground circle / single target), generated by `python3 tools/skill_shapes.py [hero…]` (probe: tools/skill_shapes_probe.js) — re-run it for a hero after changing that hero's skills or adding a new hero. No potion buttons: auto potion in items.js (⚙ AUTO POTION, HP 50% / SP 30% default). The old code-drawn battle field and blob Frostling monsters were removed 2026-11-19 (curMap null = empty bench arena only).
- Create Character (char_create.js, owner 2026-11-19, 12 Tails style): first visit (no hero saved) and 'start new game' open it. Step 1 = hero carousel (portrait = Basic skill-tree art for now; owner will send real portraits — the 7 heroes without tree art show a 'being drawn' box), Step 2 = name + base-stat roll with radar + the hero's own idle sprite (turnable). 'Create Hero' on another hero reloads as that hero (af_cc_pending) so its sprite is loaded. The name is saved in af_prog_v1_<hero>.name and kept on the HUD. Test scripts: the 🧪 button has data-m=test. Select stage v2 (owner 2026-11-24 'ทำหน้าเลือกตัวแบบ12หาง … ตัวไหนไม่ได้เลือกจะเป็นเงาๆขาวดำอยู่ด้านหลัง', his 15 full-body pictures + a 12 Tails screenshot that is NOT in the repo): step 1 = a stage (#ccs) with every hero as a figure (.ccf = grey img .g + colour img .c): the chosen one big and in colour (breathing, soft floor shadow, land-coloured glow behind: LANDC), offsets ±1 / ±2 = pale black-and-white shadows behind, bigger and cut by the frame, |off| ≥ 3 hidden; place() sets transform / opacity per figure from the stage size (wide · short = phone sideways (H < 400) · tall = W/H < 1.15, card below), switching = a .6 s carousel slide + colour crossfade (‹ ›, swipe, ← → keys, Enter = Create Hero, Esc = close when closable, tap a shadow, dots); info card (land · class, text, A / B role chips, Create Hero) + the name in Fondamento italic (assets/fonts/Fondamento-Italic-latin.woff2, OFL) with the class under it. Pictures assets/heroes/<hero>/select.webp (720×960, one scale; cut by `python3 -I tools/select_cut.py assets/heroes hero=<png>` + add the key to SELA; a big pose on a cream design sheet: `python3 -I tools/bigpose_cut.py SHEET OUT.png` first · a replaced picture gets a number in SELV (cache)); hippo = its tree art in a soft vignette, raven = a '?' silhouette until their pictures come. Tests (not in the repo): /home/claude/work/ccsel/tools cc.py OUT W H [touch=1 dpr=2 steps=s,n,p,k<i>,m<ms>] · clip.py OUT.mp4 W H · flow.py (first visit → another hero → reload → form → play, reopen closable).
- Promotion line: animal-hero MMORPG from 5 mythic lands (Norse, Greek, Egyptian, Japanese, Aztec), inspired by 12 Tails and Ragnarok, made together with AI.

Bench: wolf ATK 2,480 · TAL 2,424 (CHAR_COEF A .93, B 2.86); axolotl 2,445 (coef 2.62). C tiers are now Lv 55/60/70/80 for every hero (CTIER_LV).

Every other hero: skill_enh.js (table per hero, each set in the hero's own style; kinds echo/splash/vs/cc/sp/heal/shield/cdr/buff/res; list in SKILL_ENH_TH.md). Trees are now ~85–100 levels. Re-benched all 2026-11-19 and coefficients retuned: jaguar {A .43, B 2.45, basic .95} (its bench is noisy ±7%), owl A .78 B .75, cat .37, croc 2.95, rhino 5.14, sheep 2.95, hippo B 1.09, lion A 1.72, KI_SCALE {1.64,.88}, BA_SCALE {2.02,.415}, ANU_SCALE A .72 (now {A .60, B 1.28} after the UCore fix, see Anubis below). New skills use code SVG icons until painted ones exist.
- Monster debuffs on the player (monsters.js) fixed to the rules 2026-11-19: every 4th landed hit (no 25% roll); poison/burn/bleed = monster lv × 1.2/1/.8 HP per s; armour crack = damage taken + lv × .5 per hit; weaken = ATK/TAL − lv × .3 (through ENH_FLAT / ENH_ABSORB); slow = move −60; SP drain = lv × .4.
- Maybe lower skill coefficients (now up to ×10) by lowering monster HP instead.

- Field maps (2026-11-19): chunks bake with a 32 px margin (BP in maps/fieldmap.js) and draw 1 px overlapped → no seam lines; bake budget ≈ 6 ms/frame + one chunk pre-baked just outside the view; map colour grade (charFilter) is baked into monster sheets once (MSPR_FILTER / fsrc in monster-sprites.js) instead of ctx.filter per monster (was the biggest frame cost); spawns ×1.5 with 2–3 monsters per point (~110 per field).
- Feel pass (owner 2026-11-19, vfx_layer.js TV.feel, 11 painted heroes): visual effects live ×1.45 and are ×1.18 bigger (moving streaks keep their life), particles ×1.35, a dark halo behind each painted piece so pale FX read on snow, a charge-up glow + pulled-in sparks during every skill wind-up (startAction hook), hit-stop ×1.45 (max 150 ms), shake ×1.3. Damage timing untouched. Camera on maps: Z = clamp(min(VW,VH·1.4)/520, .95, 1.25), MAP_CHAR .92; mobile buttons /480.
- Field size (owner 2026-11-19, scaleField in maps/fieldmap.js): every outdoor field (not towns, walled caves, mazes) is laid out ×1.5 (4800×3600 → 7200×5400; all config coordinates scaled; cfg.fieldScale overrides), groves thr +.05, propDens ×1.5, openTrees ×.6, up to 5 extra landmarks copied from the map's own set pieces in new clearings, spawn points scale with area (~160 monsters).

- Effect editor (owner 2026-11-19: "make me a tuner, I'll set each skill myself"): fx_editor.js, menu ✨ (key F, or ?fxedit=1). Panel on the right (camera shifts the hero left via window.CAM_OXP). Per hero / per skill (wolf also 'basic'): pieces = picture pieces (ground / stand / streak / spark, any picture from any hero folder; list in fx_manifest.js — re-run `python3 tools/fx_manifest.py` after adding pictures) · swing (wolf painted slash that follows the axe head, PSlash in char_wolf_vfx.js) · ring / debris / dust / shake. "ปิดเอฟเฟคเดิม" (FXED.off(key)) turns the hero's own effects off — only char_wolf_vfx.js checks it so far (AT, charge, hit sparks, watchers, swing); "เส้นตามอาวุธ" toggles the weapon trail. - Effect studio (owner 2026-11-19: "a page only for making skills, wolf first, one skill at a time, you give me the attack timing"): fxedit.html → index.html?char=wolf&map=vikF2&fxstudio=1 — enters test mode, hides the HUD, opens the editor, keeps one dummy 95 px in front (hp 1e9, hero hp/sp refilled). Opening a wolf skill there starts it with old effects off. ▶ measures the attack timing (real seconds from cast to each hit, ▲ contact, ■ end) and shows it as a timeline; pieces can fire on 'โจมตีครั้งที่ n' (when 'hitN'). - Axe Cyclone (owner 2026-11-20: damage came up slowly and the effect was wider than the hits): ticks every 0.11 s instead of 0.22 with damage per tick × CY_K (old tick count / new) so the spin's total is unchanged (lv3: 15 × 0.432 = 8 × 0.81), tick remainder carried, knock-back per tick 90 → 20 so enemies stay inside, painted storm width 200 → 176 px = the real hit circle (82 + half an enemy). Bench wolf unchanged within noise.
- Vampiric Axe heal bug fixed 2026-11-20: its heal event was timed at tI+0.6 s, after the action's end (dur+recover ≈ 0.82 s), so it never fired — no HP and no '+N' number. Now at min(tI+.25, total−.03). Wolf A therefore really heals now (Lv × (1+.5·lv) per cast that hits); damage bench unchanged, survivability is higher than every earlier bench assumed.

How it hooks in: the croc's old effect classes are REBOUND (SpearLine, JawSnap, TailArc, SpearArc, PierceCone, ArmorCrack, DragMarks, RollRing, PathCrack, TipFlash, PierceTrail, SunSpearArt, MawArt, CrSpearHitArt → build the v3 piece, return an empty object; bronzeHit / dirtKick / mudSplash replaced), make() wrappers add charge-ups, early jaws, sun stone, spirit, ambush wake …; hit sparks come from a wrapper on `cr` (Armor Break read before/after → a cash-in shatters + Cipactli seal). Pieces: Lance (blade reaches the target exactly on the contact frame, lead = rt(.03)), AirCone, BodyCrack, Jaws (open early, shut on the contact frame; mode 'lock' stays shut while CR.grab holds that prey; shake() for Violent Shake), TailSwing (passes straight behind on the hit frame), Whirl, Wake, Furrow, GCrack, ObsSpike, Spirit. Checked: hit times of all 35 casts identical old vs new (tools/fxlab.py hits), herosweep + combo clean, ?vfx3=0 clean. BUG FIXED (gameplay): enterMap() clears fx and the game enters its first map right after the croc installs, so CrxCore + CrOverlay were dead from the start (Jaw Lock / Death Roll never held the prey, Armor Break / Blood Frenzy never decayed, Ancient Maw alternation carried over, gauges never drawn) → crEnsure() in char_croc.js re-adds them (and the v3 watchers via CRV.ensure) on every cast / basic, and a CrKeeper on layer 'none' (enterMap keeps those, same pattern as AxKeeper) calls it every frame; window.crEnsure (bench probes pick up *Ensure). - Central market v2 (owner 2026-11-21: 'demand / supply like ROM — many buyers = price up; scarce item listed = 1-hour timer, buyers reserve, more buyers than items = random winners and the price goes up'; then 'no daily cap, let it grow as far as demand / supply takes it'; then 'ตามนี้' = go). Rules + status: MARKET_SYSTEM_TH.md. PHASE 1 DONE: every stackable good (ETC: mats, ores, orbs, crystals, potions / food, tools — 162 items) trades in market.js; AFItems.openMarket() opens it (window #mkx, tabs ซื้อ / ลงขาย / ของฉัน / กระดานราคา + '⚔ อุปกรณ์' = the OLD seller-priced market in items.js, now gear only, openGearMarket). The system sets one central price per item (sellers choose only the quantity). Stock = instant buy; bought out = +5% and 'out of stock'; the next listing opens a 60-minute reservation round (price locked, full price held, 1 reservation per hero per round, 1 unit each in turn then lots, losers refunded, results wait in the claim box). After a round the price moves by demand ÷ units: none −3%, short −1%, 1× +2%, +6% per doubling, max +20%; normal sale: hourly 5% × (bought − listed) ÷ (bought + listed) (needs 5 units), overstock −2% per 6 h. NO daily cap, NO ceiling; floor 65% of the items.js price; goods in SHOP_FULL never above the shop price. Fees 1% listing (not refunded) + 5% tax; 10 listings, 48 h; what you won in a round cannot be listed for 24 h; a seller cannot reserve in his own round. market.js part 1 = engine without DOM (makeMarket, crowdDef = how many simulated sellers / buyers an hour per class of goods, NEWS = market news that multiplies demand or supply of one group for 5–10 h, waiting buyers: 60% come back when a round opens); part 2 = glue + window. ONE world per save mode shared by all heroes (localStorage af_mkt2_play / af_mkt2_test, ≈ 85 KB; W.it = items, W.pl[hero] = listings / reservations / box / log); a brand-new hero (AFItems.fresh) gets its record cleared; old stackable listings of the old market are returned through the claim box. The market runs on real time, also while the game is closed (catch-up ≤ 7 days, new world = 7 days of warm-up). Test mode shows ⏩ +10 min / +1 h / +1 day buttons (W.skew) — use them (or MKT.skip(ms)) to try a round; `?market=1` opens the window by itself (test mode anywhere, play mode in capT). Debug: window.MKT {M (engine), world(), skip, save, reset}. Checks: `node tools/market_check.js` (rule tests on the real engine + price tables + 30 days of the whole market; re-run after changing K, crowdDef or NEWS and update section 14 of the doc); browser scripts in /home/claude/work/market (mshot.py, final.py, persist.py). NOT done: gear lots (phase 2), real players (phase 3, needs the server), price board outside the capital (no button yet), Lv 20 gate for reserving, portrait phone check, owner's verdict on the feel (he may want more crowded rounds → raise NEWS dem / K.WAIT).

- Cat icons v2 (owner 2026-11-21): all 64 cat skill icons repainted from 6 sheets (slot → key table in CAT_ICON_PROMPTS_TH.md, cut by /home/claude/work/cuticons.py), 8 spare icons in assets/icons/cat/spare/. sw.js V bumped to tl-v2 so cached old icons refresh.
- Smoothness pass (owner 2026-11-21 'ปรับให้เกมลื่นๆทุกแมพ'). How to measure + the rules: tools/perf/README.md (the scripts are in the repo now). The frame is drawing-bound, not script-bound (~700 monsters cost 0.3 ms of script). Measured with software canvas at 1280×720: field frame 64.6 → 22.7 ms (Low 51.6 → 16.3), capital 27.8, dungeon 12.5, craft hall 48.5 → 20.5; worst frame when new ground is painted 300–550 → 50–85 ms; picture memory after 10 maps 1,135 MB (tab crashed after ~17 maps) → flat 200–400 MB over all 101 maps. What changed:
  · maps/fieldmap.js: `inLand` asks only the 'big' pieces (map layout ~2 s faster); the shadows of a chunk are laid on one scratch canvas and blurred in ONE draw (was ~100 filtered draws a chunk = the walking stutter); ground tints fill only their own box; `SCR()` = every 'screen' pass of `atmosphere()` (light shafts, drifting glows) goes into one quarter-size black canvas blended once — new glows must use it; vignette cached; big soft blits use imageSmoothingQuality 'low'; `LITE()` = Low quality drops light shafts / glows / cloud shadows (colour mood stays); host.release() gives back chunks, scratch canvases and silhouettes.
  · index.html: `layerDirty` — the FX layer is blended and bloomed only in frames where something was drawn into it (lctx draw calls are wrapped; beginLayer in vfx_rework.js resets it); bloom's two blends merged into one; enterMap releases every other map and keeps only MAP_KEEP laid-out maps (2 on desktop, 1 on phones / ≤4 GB) — older ones are rebuilt on entry (same layout, seeded); auto quality: High → Med under ~42 fps, Med → Low under ~28 fps, single long frames and the first 2.5 s of a map are not counted, two slow periods in a row needed.
  · fashion.js `harmonized`: outline + filtered body are composed on a small off-screen canvas (never a filter on the game canvas) and at a capped resolution (180k px) when the hero is drawn large (rooms, sharp phone screens). monster-sprites.js: graded sheets (`FSH`) are freed when the colour grade changes; sheets are decoded before first use.
  · Checked: layouts identical (object lists) and painted ground within 9/255 of before (tools/perf/cmp.py), atmosphere compared on all 27 moods (fg.py), kitsune bench unchanged (2,459 vs 2,444), all 101 maps entered in one session without errors (sweep.py).
  · Left: each field still holds ~140 MB of processed sprites (artPass + variant + baseAO canvases and their ImageBitmaps in fieldmap.js mk(); the `variant` canvases are only needed as input of baseAO); heroes without painted icons request them anyway (bastet 55, sheep 64, ex_ icons of hippo / croc / jaguar / owl / rhino / axolotl = 404s, harmless — a manifest would need re-running by every chat that adds icons, so it was not done).
- Long-term plan for gear / refine / gems (owner 2026-11-21 'คิด ทำให้มันยาวนานหลากหลาย ระบบไอเทม ตีบวก โดยเฉพาะเจม'): GEAR_GEM_LONGTERM_TH.md + tools/longterm_sim.py (numbers in the doc come from it). A PLAN ONLY, nothing built: refine without breaking up to +15 (fail = −1 + 'cracked', repaired with a same tier / slot piece, +5% pity per fail, safe refine, star ore from bosses), reforge with a locked line, city sets 2/4/6, 30 boss pieces, codex, orb sets; gems = raw stones → carve (choose the gem, roll quality, pity 20) → 18 random lines with ranks I–V (incl. 'board link' lines) → levels 1–10 (6–10 need duplicates, Lv 10 = second mutation socket) → later resonance, boss legend gems, 3 presets, minor stars. Owner answered 2026-11-21 → parts A + B BUILT (next note); the rest is still a plan.
- Bug sweep (owner 2026-11-21 'ตรวจหาบั๊ก ความผิดพลาดในเกม'), scripts in tools/qa (README there). FIXED: (1) Bastet's core ticker returned nothing → dropped after one frame: hex damage, locust swarms, obelisk SP, blessing heal, army end and shield expiry only ran on the frame of a cast, and every cast left one more BaGround + BaTop behind (slower the longer she was played); she also benched far under target (A 2,088 · B 1,690) → fixed + BA_SCALE {A 1.38, B .70} → A 2,467 · B 2,428. (2) Melee skills WITHOUT a `range` (anubis anLunge, dragon dcBreath + dcAbyss, lion lcToss) never fired from a key press / tap when an enemy was near: useSkill queued 'walk up, then cast' and 'distance <= undefined' is never true, so the hero walked into the enemy and stood there (the bench casts directly, so it never showed). Now only melee skills with a range walk up first; the queued-cast check tolerates a missing range. RULE: a `type:'melee'` skill that should approach its target needs `range`; run tools/qa/ranges.py after adding skills. CLEAN: all 101 maps (load, frames, monsters, hero not in a wall), every warp leads to an existing map and back, 251 monster kinds all have sprites + files, every skill of all 14 heroes through the real key path (combo.py), combo follow-ups, basic attacks, menus / shortcut keys / item, market, craft windows and their tabs (wolf, cat), phone touch controls (iPhone size), first-time player in play mode (create hero → field → fight) and save / reload, 112 scripts syntax-checked, no missing literal asset paths. NOTES for the owner (not changed): a new Lv 1 hero has 20 unspent stat points and nothing points at them — until they are spent the first monster takes about a minute to kill; Escape does not close windows (the same key / button toggles them).
- Refine v2 + gem farming v2 BUILT (owner 2026-11-21 answers: 1 'it may break, but it must be repaired' · 2 '+15 yes, but very very hard' · 3 'gem level cap 50 for now, raise it in updates; gem type must be random — players go to the central market, or get shards to craft' · 4 'you decide, make it long-lasting').   · Refine (items.js REFINE_CHANCE … repairItem, index.html gearCalc/ORB_CAP/itemSt): +1–5 100 %, +6–10 90/80/65/50/40 % (fail = −1 + BROKEN), +11–15 20/12/8/5/3 % (fail = BROKEN, refine stays, +1 % pity per fail on that piece up to +10 %, it.rp) + Star Ore 'sore' ×(r−9) per try. Broken (it.broken) = no stats / sets / traits, cannot refine, move or list; repair in the refine window with a same slot + same grade piece (sameKind / repairMates) or zeny repairZ. Guard Stone = no break (still −1 below +11; ×(r−9) above +10). +12 = one extra orb hole, +15 = the piece's special stats ×1.2. Broken icons get a red crack (itemIcon wrapper in items.js).
  · Gems (gem_data.js = shared tables, loaded before items.js; totem_gems.js save v3): own[k] = {lv 1–GEM_CAP 50, q 0/1/2, lines [[id, rank 1–5, locked]], dup}; save per mode (af_gems_<hero>_play in play mode); mutation / support gems are BAG goods gmut_<id> / gsup_<id> (socketing takes one, unsocketing or taking the main gem off the board gives it back); Totem Shrine panel on the board (carve 3 stones → random gem of that tier, craft from 40 shards = chosen gem, dismantle spare → 5 shards + 3 essence); quality 70/25/5 % with pity 20; 18 line kinds working in combat (hooks in totem_gems.js hurt / castSkillNow / applyCC / ENH_ABSORB / apply); level costs essence + zeny, breakthroughs at 10/20/30/40 need spares 1/1/2/2; power +3 %/lv to 5 (unchanged) then +0.4 %/lv; Lv 50 opens a 2nd mutation socket (MODS[i][3], placed by clearance). Gems from before have empty line slots (reroll fills them) so the bench is unchanged (checked wolf + kitsune). Test mode grants every gem + goods once (d.tg). window.totemGems.key = the save key for test tools.
  · New goods (items.js ETC; market.js crowdDef + category 🔮 เจม/โทเท็ม + 2 news): sore, tstone1–4, tfrag1–4, tess, gmut_* ×12, gsup_* ×12 → market 196 goods. Drops in dropFor (rates in gem_data.js DROPS); boss kills (only Fenrir so far) give a bundle.
  · Checked: refine break → repair → +11 → pity → +12 hole → +15 bonus (scripted), board / shrine / lines / market gem tab screenshots (desktop + 844×390), all 18 lines cast with every skill of wolf, kitsune, bastet without errors, UI click-through clean, market_check OK.
  · Open → answered 2026-11-22: the character cap is 60 at launch (Level cap 60 note at the end: gem sockets / C tiers squeezed into 51–60, gem level cap 50 stays). Next phases: reforge (C), city sets / boss gear / codex / orb sets (D), resonance / boss legend gems / cross-branch gems / presets (E), market phase 2.

- Totem pages + resonance (owner 2026-11-21 'โอเค' to 'start item 3 with the 3 pages + resonance'): TOTEM_GEM_SYSTEM_TH.md §1f. PAGES: 3 bookmark tabs on top of the totem window's left column (#tgPg; names ฟาร์ม / บอส / ปาร์ตี้, tap the active tab = card: rename ≤12, copy this page over another with a 2-tap confirm, summary). A page = where every gem sits (sock + wing mods, ss / sm / ssu); the active page IS the live board (save D.pg = {c, n, L:[layout|null ×3]}, L[c] not read; old saves → page 1). switchPage(t): snapshot live → L[c]; a never-used page = copy of the current one; else every wing / gold / teal gem goes back to the bag and the page's gems are taken from it — what the bag lacks, holes not carved, skills not learned stay empty and are listed. C gems / skill gems are per hero (shared by all pages). Free switch: in a town any time, elsewhere only after PG_CALM 5 s without dealing or taking damage (FIGHT_T set in the hurt + ENH_ABSORB wrappers; tabs dim while waiting); test mode any time unless ?tgseal=1. RESONANCE: 2 working mutation gems of one kind anywhere on the totem (the board cap is 2 per kind) = that kind's GAIN one step stronger on both skills; BALANCE: no kind twice and ≥ BAL_N 5 kinds = every mutation's COST one step softer (one pair breaks it). Texts gem_data.js RESO (g / c replace MUT[4] / [5]), numbers totem_gems.js RV [normal, stronger]; RES {n, on, tot, kinds, bal} is rebuilt in apply() (counts only gems that work); RS(id) / BAL(); window.__RESO_OFF=1 turns both off (tests). Shown: board threads in the kind's colour between the pair's holes (garland down on the base, bow outward on the wings, symbol in the middle, sparks running), balance = turning gold dashed rings round every working mutation hole (resGlow on #tgLines), badges top right of the board (#tgRes: 🔗 + gem ×2 / ⚖ + kinds; tap = 12-row table), status line in the mutation tab (#tgRsb), 🔗 / ⚖ marks on bag gems, gem marks on hotbar / phone wheel get a white (rs) or gold (bl) ring, tooltips / cards / button titles show the numbers that work now (mutG / mutC). Checked: heavy pair ×1.098 per hit, rapid in balance ×1.108, fire pair 5 burn ticks, heavy in balance cd 9 → 8 SP 22 → 19, stack pair 3 charges, 14 heroes × 4 layouts cast without errors, 6 page switches restore wings / teal / skill holes exactly with bag + board totals unchanged, town / 5 s rule, reload. Bench: standard bench unchanged (no mutations there); wolf A with heavy ×2 + rage ×2 on its main skills 2,351 → 2,524 (+7 %); the greedy bench casts only the first few skills, so balance was checked per skill. Test scripts in /home/claude/work/pages (func.py, pginv.py, block.py, resweep.py, shots.py, bench_res.py, balchk.py, h.js helpers). Not done: a quick-switch button outside the window, per-page hotbar layouts.
- Gem looks on the real skill, step 1 = element gems (owner 2026-11-21 'ลุย' + 'some can be made with SVG / VFX too'): gem_fx.js (loaded right after seals.js, before every hero VFX file; ?gemfx=0 = off) — TOTEM_GEM_SYSTEM_TH.md §1g. A skill whose gem mutation is fire / ice / volt (ACT[k].mut from totem_gems.js, follow-ups included; '@auto' = basic attacks) draws in that element: every effect piece remembers its skill (addFx sets f._sk; pieces made inside castSkillNow — make(), the weapon trail — and pieces spawned by a tinted piece's update inherit it), and while a tinted piece draws, CanvasRenderingContext2D fillStyle / strokeStyle / shadowColor (incl. gradients, re-made as tinted twins) and drawImage are mapped through the element's brightness ramp (LUT: whites stay white); pictures are recoloured once (whole picture ≤1.2 MP, else per source-rect cell, canvases re-done every .5 s, 24 MP budget); the FX layer's own lctx.drawImage wrapper (layerDirty) is routed through the tint too — anything that draws into lctx must call lctx.drawImage, not the bare prototype method, or the layer is not composited; emit() particles are mapped when born. Extras tied to the real gem effects (hurt wrapper): per-hit GfxBurst (fire licks + embers / ice shards + frost ring / volt forks), GfxOv ground + top = scorch decals, flames on burning enemies (e._gBurn), frost + crystals while the ice slow lasts (e._gfI), ice block while frozen (own timer e._gfF / e._gfFe, works on test dummies) + shatter, volt crackle (e._gfV); SVG art rasterised once in gem_fx.js. No gameplay change; frame time unchanged (wolf test room). Lasting looks drawn by a hero's shared watcher (zones, buffs, banners) are NOT tinted yet: wrap them per hero with GEMFX.tint(key,()=>draw) / GEMFX.spawn(key,()=>addFx…). Tools: tools/gems/tintcmp.py (stills, real=1 puts the mutation on skill hole 1), tintclip.py (2x2 mp4), gemfx_sweep.py (every skill, errors) — all 14 heroes swept clean. Next: per-hero watcher wiring, then step 2 (other mutations: heavy, rapid, focus, spread, guard, rage, siphon, stack), step 3 (supports), step 4 (element-coloured skill buttons).
- Gem looks on the real skill, step 2 = the other nine mutation gems (owner 2026-11-21 'ได้ ทำเอฟเฟคให้เห็นชัดๆสวยๆว่าเอฟเฟคได้รับการเปลี่ยนแล้ว'): gem_mut_fx.js (loaded right after gem_fx.js, uses its kit GEMFX.k; ?gemfx=0 = off) — TOTEM_GEM_SYSTEM_TH.md §1g-2. Each gem has one signature look, drawn when its rule really works and sized by the real numbers: focus = gold lock-on reticle on R.first + piercing flare (others a faint glance) · spread = orange shock ring out to the real 80 radius + sparks to every enemy the splash reaches · heavy = wind-up ring sinking into the feet + ⬇ chevrons, each hit a falling wedge + hot ground crack + rocks + dust + shake(1.2) · rapid = green ≫ chevrons + wind lines at the cast, thin green cross-cuts on hits · stack = crystal tokens orbiting the hero = STK[k].ch (thrown at the target on a cast, pop in on a refill) · guard = glass hex bubble while GU.sh holds (flash when hit, cracks by depletion, shatters on break, floor ring during guardT) · rage = crimson spike burst, then for GU.rageT a rotating crimson floor sigil + flames behind the hero + wisps, cracks when the hero loses HP · siphon = a mana crystal from each enemy that really returned SP (REC[k].sp grew) + '+N SP' · totem = carved totem pole SVG (thunderbird / owl / bear) rising from the ground, dashed gold circle = its real 150 reach, eyes flash + gold pulse + beams to every 'TOTEM' hit, 4 pips = casts left, cracks and sinks. totem_gems.js now exports totemGems.fxs() → {REC,STK,GU,PIL} (read only) and skips its plain ring() pulses and the old box pillar when GEMFX.mutOn / GEMFX.pole are set. Own pieces carry __gt '-' (never element-tinted); hit looks are rate-limited per enemy; the lasting looks draw from three pieces (ground / sorted / top) that exist only while needed (a 'top' piece makes the FX layer work every frame) + a 'none' watcher that survives enterMap. Tools: tools/gems/mutcmp.py (stills, gem socketed for real, ev=SECONDS:<js> for scripted moments) and tools/gems/mutclip.py (3×3 grid video of all nine, the bubble drained and broken, the raging hero hurt). Hit times differ per skill (wolf Blood Cleave hits ~0.73 s after the cast) — capture after the hit. Step 3 done (next note); next: step 4 skill buttons (charge / pillar / third-cast counters, element colours).
- Gem looks on the real skill, step 3 = the 12 support gems (owner 2026-11-21 'ทำไรต่อ'): gem_sup_fx.js (right after gem_mut_fx.js; uses GEMFX.k + GEMFX.mk exported by the two earlier files; ?gemfx=0 = off) — TOTEM_GEM_SYSTEM_TH.md §1g-3. Drawn when the rule works, half-strength (skill-hole) supports ×0.8: echo = violet countdown ring ⟲ closing on the enemy for the 0.5 s, then on the gem's own 'ECHO' hit (gh, _gem — the wolf's Savage Echo also labels 'ECHO' and is ignored) a violet ghost of the hero in its first-hit pose lunges at the enemy (FX layer) + ghost cut + ripples · third = ①②③ pips over the head from A.casts, 3rd merges and bursts, its hits flash amber rays · force = ✚ spark per hit · splash = floor splat + drops to the real radius (70, 80 with spread) + dashed ring · chain = every GemZap (also the volt gem's bounce) is swapped in an addFx wrapper for a ChainBolt (jagged filled bolt, cyan / violet with volt) · quick = green clock dial under the hero at the cast · thrift = 3 mana diamonds pop over the head and drop back · burn = fire lick per hit, enemies with e._gFlat get two small flames in gem_fx.js GfxOv (call GEMFX.k.ensureOv) · frost = A.n%3 ice burst + GEMFX.mark ice, %6 freeze block · pierce = silver lance through enemies that HAVE def + plates · leech = hearts from R.heal growth + '+N HP' (real HP gained) · impact = A.n%5 comic star + ground ring. Buff side: addBuff wrapper shows the gem pictures (assets/gems/support) + 'บัฟ +N วิ' for echo/force/chain/pierce; 0.95 s after a cast whose R has no hits → splash / burn / frost / leech / impact looks round the hero. totem_gems.js: third counts only casts that happen (A.casts=nc after ok), burn tick numbers rounded. NOTE for tests: totemGems.apply() rebuilds ACT (counters 0) whenever D changes — the first apply after a socket change can be followed by one more on the next 400 ms tick (lazy skill-gem data), so tests call apply() twice before casting. Tools: tools/gems/supcmp.py (stills), supclip.py (4×3 grid clip, 3 casts; tag=_a/_b nogrid=1 in parallel then grid=_a,_b — one 12-cell clip takes ~25 min alone), supsweep.py (every skill × 2 supports × 3 casts).
- Gem looks on the real skill, step 4 = the skill BUTTONS (owner 2026-11-22 'ทำต่อเลย'): gem_btn.js (after gem_sup_fx.js; ?gemfx=0 = off; window.GEMBTN {tick, scan, off, on}) — TOTEM_GEM_SYSTEM_TH.md §1g-4. Buttons carry data-k (index.html buildHotbar, mobile.js build). Every 50 ms it reads totemGems.act() / fxs() and gives each gem skill's button a live overlay, rewritten only when its state string changes: element mutation = animated element frame + inner glow (fire: 3 flames licking up from the bottom edge + heat gradient · ice: shimmer sweep + crystals at the bottom corners · volt: flickering frame + zaps on the edges) · stack = hex crystals under the button (STK ch of mx, the next one fills by rT) · third = gold pips from A.casts%3, before the 3rd a gold pulsing frame + '×1.5' inside (hidden while the combo prompt shows) · totem = while PIL holds the skill (not the balance last cast) a dark layer + totem badge with casts left + gold conic ring to the next pillar cast · rage = red pulsing frame while GU.rageT · guard = silver frame + shield badge with the shield amount · support gems = their pictures down the left edge (skill-hole ones smaller). Layers: .gbo (z 2: dim + ring, under the cooldown number), .gbf (z 5: everything else — at z 2 the hotbar neighbours' badges covered half of the ring), .gbb (z −1, unused for now). Checked PC hotbar + phone wheel (wolf, cat, kitsune; tools/gems/btnshot.py TAG [set=A|B], TOUCH=1 w=844 h=390 for the phone; harness env KEEPHUD=1 / TOUCH=1).

Pictures assets/loading/ld1–8.webp (1672 px, q80) + ld<n>_s.webp (960 px) — a random one each load, never the same twice in a row (localStorage af_boot_last); landscape = cover, portrait (max-aspect-ratio 5/4) = the whole picture over a blurred copy. Bottom: one random tip (TIPS, keep them true) + the totem app icon + a gold bar. READY = document no longer 'loading' (every script ran; waiting for 'complete' also waited for the pictures of hidden windows — 11 s more on a slow line) + mapHost().ready, +350 ms so the first chunks paint, or 12 s without any progress → 0.45 s fade, element removed, window.__bootDone=1. Files: axolotl_art.js (26 SVG pieces → window.KTA: drops in 3 colours, bubbles ×2, lotus, lotus petals = the shared petal0–2, leaves, sprout, healing cross, tide sigil = gold jade-bracelet ring + 4 teal gems + water band + drops + lotus rosette, water hand, heart of water, pink gill frond, crown splash ×3, curling wave ×2, water-saw disc, water drill ×3 frames, water cocoon, water lifebuoy) · axolotl_vfx.js (uses the K3 + VK kits: the guards of wolf_vfx3.js and cat_vfx.js now include 'axolotl'; cat_vfx.js loads the cat's painted masks only for the cat). The axolotl's skill code is inline in index.html, so the VFX file repaints its classes through their PROTOTYPES (update = game, draw = new; extras spawned on the first frame / the hit frame): AxSprayFx (every spray drop = SVG drop leaning into its motion, bubbles float, gill breaths are bubbles), Ripple, Splash (crown + puddle + flare), AxBolt, AxDropFly, WaterJet (aimed from the raised hand down to the body of the first enemy on the line, then level: needle / compression marble / big turbulent cannon + wet floor), PressureOrb, WaterDrill, WaterHand (+3 waves on the slap), WaterSaw (+wet groove), WaterRing (collapse = wall of water, back half on the ground layer, front half sorted → geyser at contact; gyro = whirlpool floor + 3 bands round the target), Membrane, BubbleLift, PopBubble, MintGlints, Helix (2.5D ribbon, back half dimmer), WaterLotus (owner's painted lotus kept + tide sigil + bloom flash), WaterTrail, HeartOrb (+WHeartFloor, a wave ring per beat, burst = geyser + crown + mist), HarmonyMist, PuddleRun, FloatBubble (lifebuoy), GillGlow, TideCircle (cast-bar sigil), Cocoon, AxOverlay.draw (Living Water drops). Wrapped: axCast (water gathers at the hand for the wind-up, a ring closes, flash on release), mkBasic (the 'buff' cast clip instead of the axe swing, same release time and length), jgAfterHurt (hit splash), axHeal (one pulse per real heal), spray() ×0.6 (visual only), frontArc off; addFx / emit filters drop HitCrescents / PuffDust / round sparks and turn the ex_ skills' '#ffe9a8' rings / flashes into water (also when they fire from the key press). Buff pieces dedupe (newest wins). ?vfx3=0 = old code look. Visual timing fixes: PressureOrb and HeartOrb lives were in action seconds (the orb vanished before the release; the ult's third-beat burst never showed). BUG FIXED (gameplay, index.html): AxOverlay + AxSprayFx were made once at load and died on the first enterMap (it clears fx) → HoTs never healed, Living Water stopped after one drop, Rebound / Overflow / Last Drop worked once, gill SP regen was off, the shared-breath shield never expired, Second Spring's cocoon never ended, spray drops were never drawn. axEnsure() + AxKeeper (layer 'none', survives enterMap) re-add them; every maker calls axEnsure() (the bench clears fx). Bench (whole kit, test mode, /home/claude/work/ax/bench_ax.py, same method gives the cat 2,375): VFX on = off (2,732 both); after the fix 2,733 (healPS 433 → 510); the axolotl was ~11 % over target already (not retuned since the ex_ skills) → CHAR_COEF.axolotl 3.02 → 2.71 → 2,451 (8 runs). Test tools in /home/claude/work/ax: harness.py (manual rAF stepping + pre-seeded test mode, so the test room never reloads), strip.py TAG keys [every n cols scale w h dx dy skip mode=one|room], custom.py TAG "<js>", timing.py, clip.py basic|a|b|c, perf.py, bench_ax.py. Not done: phone check, owner's verdict (own sprite since 2026-11-21, see Axolotl sprite). SAME BUG in the rhino's RhOverlay (momentum decay, shield expiry, ICDs, the shield dome) — FIXED 2026-11-22 ('แก้บัค 1'): rhEnsure() + RhKeeper (layer 'none') in index.html, every rhino maker / basic calls rhEnsure(). The croc's CrxCore / CrOverlay were fixed 2026-11-21 (crEnsure + CrKeeper, see Croc effects v3).
- Axolotl sprite (owner 2026-11-21, 20 clips 'สไปร้แอคเซอลอท'): assets_axolotl.js (prefix AXL, ≈5.5 MB, loaded only for ?char=axolotl like the dragon) built by tools/cliphero.py from tools/clips/axolotl_plan.json (clip paths = that chat's uploads; uploads came buff, attack, walk, idle per direction S, SE, E, NE, N). index.html: AXL_ART swaps the atlas in (atkRemap {}), CHAR_FILTER '' (no pink tint), CHAR_SCALE 1.57 (idle height vs the wolf, same method gives the dragon 1.64). Attack (contact 6) and buff (peak 7) frames are picked per direction, their frame TIMES are the wolf sprite's E timings in every direction → each cast / basic keeps the release time it had facing E (before, S / N / SE / NE followed the wolf's own slower or faster clips). Sockets = the held water orb found per frame (cliphero 'orb'), so water now leaves from the orb, not an axe socket; axolotl_vfx.js no longer swaps the basic attack to the buff clip when AXL_ART. cliphero.py gained topFade / sideFade / strict / dur / orb / feetFit (usage at the top of the file). Compromises: the N buff clip has a white flash and its camera zooms out from frame 15 — frames 16–17 are keyed with the strict matte and scaled back ×1.08 / ×1.15 by their feet width (cliphero feetFit; the owner saw the hero shrink in the back-facing cast, every other clip was checked: no zoom), the lowering reuses the raise frames reversed; the S / SE / E buff water ring is wider than the video and fades at the sides. Walk plays at the clip's own pace (cycle 1.08–1.25 s, the dragon's is 1.0–1.2 s, the wolf's 0.8 s) — speed it up if the owner sees skating. Checked: hit times of 26 casts: 20 identical, 6 first hits ~1 frame earlier (basic, axPop, first hits of axDrill / axSaw / axCompress / axCannon — projectiles start at the orb, nearer the target), herosweep 0 errors (its 'basic damage 0' is the test room refilling dummy HP, not a bug), leak flat, bench same session 2,525 new / 2,499 old (noise, CHAR_COEF unchanged). Showcase clip for any hero's sprite: HERO=<h> OUT=dir python3 tools/fx/spriteclip.py (turnaround, walk circle, basic in 4 directions, CASTS=key|label,…). HUD portrait still the default face.

How it hooks in: the dragon's own classes are repainted through their prototypes (DgWave = wind palm / seal palm / ki fist / Thousand Waves / Abyssal Torrent / ki dragons by kindOf(col,w,kind); DgBolt draws nothing and DBolt + boltHit are spawned when the game adds it; DgArc = gold chain or Mirror Pond's water return; DgGround + DgTop = clouds, rain, mirror, Still Mind ring, pearls / coil dragons / awakening flames / sovereign cloud split behind (ground layer) and in front (top), flow drops, seals). dPow and dSplash are rebound (hit flashes by element from the colour). Punch fists: each make() is wrapped, the first n action events are the punches and a DFist lands exactly on each event (lead = rt(event − now)). - Lion effects v3 (owner 2026-11-21 'ทำของสิงโตต่อ', same brief as the dragon): plan + per-skill table + timings in LION_FX_PLAN_TH.md. Files: lion_art.js (25 SVG pieces → window.KTA: pankration fists fistG / fistB = white fur in brown leather thongs + gold knuckle plate + gold cuff with the bolt (B = lightning-charged), grab = reaching gold paw with claws, paw = gold paw emblem in a half laurel wreath, laurel / laurelG / crownL / leaf, meander + meanderZ = Greek-key floor ring (Z = Zeus bolt disc in the middle), slab0–2 marble slabs, crater = cracked marble floor, column (Doric), javelin (lightning shaft, gold head), keraunos (Zeus's double bolt), eagle0/1 (golden eagle, wings up / down), aegisE = hoplite shield with a lion face, lionHead = roaring white lion spirit, wing = lightning-feather wing, storm = slate cloud with gold rims, zeus = bolt sigil, medal = glory medal) · lion_vfx.js (window.LIV; uses K3 + VK — 'lion' added to the guards of cat_vfx.js and wolf_vfx3.js; include order lion_art.js after dragon_art.js, before cat_vfx.js; lion_vfx.js after dragon_vfx.js). How it hooks in: lPow / lSlamFx rebound on window (lSlamFx still calls the original so shake + hit-stop stay; LV3.slam marks its ring / dust to be swallowed); the lion's classes repainted through their prototypes (LiShot = javelin / Keraunos + lightning wake, LiEagle = SVG eagle facing its flight, LiArc = ribbon lightning, LiBolt draws nothing — the addFx filter spawns DBolt + boltHit instead (from the cloud's height when it comes from LI.cloud), LiGround / LiTop = cloud shadow, grip ring round the held enemy (back half on the ground layer), glory medals, 10 charge bolts, aegis dome, Aetos eagle orbit, Ascension wings, Hide / Rest / Crown pieces, the storm cloud art at z150); LiFling gets DFling (gold trail) + DFlingShadow; engine rings (GRing → SoftRing) are exact-time markers (ringMark: Nemean Hide, Laurel Rest, King's Roar, Static Body, Sky Strike, Spin turns, Overcharge, Aegis, King of the Gods, Laurel Crown, Ascension); punches() lands a fist / paw exactly on each punch event; casts gather power at the hand for the wind-up (DGather); Arena Spin = DWhirl (gold + dust streaks, back half on the ground layer); Shove = DPush (force crescent rolling forward); Gaia's Crack = DCrack line; Labors = meander ring + 8 DColumns + ghost + streak per teleport; Counter Hold = DShield. Pieces born on a hit start PRE (.035 s) into their life so they are lit while hit-stop freezes the frame. emit filter: the lion's marble dust puffs ('#e8e0d0' / '#c8b8a0') become ground-coloured K3 dust; round / spark / ember dropped in skill context. char_lion_fx.js (painted) runs only with ?vfx3=0. Checked: hit times of all 23 skills + basic identical old vs new (only periodic Static Field / cloud ticks differ in phase: LiCore is layer 'none', made at install, so its phase depends on load time), herosweep 0 errors, leak.py flat, combo.py no errors (its 0.8 s window is shorter than Suplex / Lift's hits at 0.72–0.75 s → 'no hit' there is the test, not the game). BUGS FIXED (gameplay): (1) LiCore.update returned nothing → ticker dead after one frame (glory / charge never expired, grab never held, Rest / Crown never healed, Static Field never shocked, Storm Cloud never struck or ended, Supercell never followed) → return CHAR==='lion' + lEnsure() (window.lEnsure, re-added on every make and basic). (2) Events after the action's end are never fired (the engine ends the action at dur+recover and drops what is left): Lion Pounce's clip ended at .267 s, before its landing at .32 → it never hit (clip now lasts T+.15); Arena Spin's clip ended .15 s before its release → no throw, no glory, no Atlas Slam ever (one more .15 s hold); Wrath of Olympus's buff clip ends ~1.0 s but its 12 bolts ran to 2.5 s → 4 bolts and never King of the Gods (bolts are now setFxTimer timers from the release, same rhythm gap/ACTION_TEMPO, the lion can move). Probe: tools/qa/lostev.py [hero…] (makes every skill next to a dummy and compares each event time with dur / dur+recover). Bastet, anubis, dragon, axolotl, sheep, hippo, owl, rhino clean. (3) skyDarkT stayed dark forever after Wrath (and after the dragon's Azure Dragon Descends) → reset to 0 at the end. Bench (TAL preset, per branch, 8 runs): before A 2,449 · B 1,235 → core fix A 2,592 · B 1,675 → event fixes A 2,242 · B 3,026 → CHAR_COEF.lion A 1.61 → 1.66, B .814 → .96 → A 2,470 · B 2,462 (effects off A 2,458 · B 2,456). - World boss (owner 2026-11-22 'อยากทำเวิลบอสที่ให้ทุกคนทั้งเซิฟมารุมตี ช่วยออกแบบบอสตัวแรกให้หน่อย คอนเซปอะไรก็ได้'): PROPOSAL ONLY, nothing built — WORLD_BOSS_TH.md. Urtok the Totem-Bearer = an ancient giant tortoise carrying the broken Great Totem stump + 5 land crystal pillars on its shell (no green: turquoise instead of moss). Loop: legs count landed HITS (crit 2) → global flip gauge → it flips on its back → crystal heart on its chest takes damage 20 s (+4 s per broken pillar), body / shell / head immune → it rights itself (arena quake, heal check) + shell spin → repeat (~3 flips, 20 min limit, enrage at 16). Gem question 'legs count hits, heart counts damage' (rapid vs heavy / focus; totem pages tie-in). Jobs per RO stat: STR / AGI legs, LUK crits, INT pillars (ranged only, low MDEF), DEX shoots shard wisps (high FLEE) that repair legs, VIT guardian ring at the head (bite + breath only hit the ring while someone stands in it), healers the quake. Damage = target Lv × k (no percents, no random: targets by counters). Pillar broken → that land's seal animal is freed = flat server-wide blessing. Scaling N (level-weighted, min 20 filled by AI 'spirit warriors'). Rewards by contribution, no last-hit bonus; relic 'Totem Shell' (first relic that protects allies). Server options: offline with AI (now) → async shared world HP via a small server → real-time rooms (Colyseus). Replaces the Aether Wyrm placeholder at the guild master (npcs.js guildMenu). Art prompt in the doc §16. Waiting for the owner's verdict + answers to §17. 2026-11-22: the owner sent the master picture (no words) → assets/bosses/world/urtok.png (raw on green, like the other bosses) + urtok_cut.webp (keyed, trimmed 1166×1234); in-map scale mock = tools/fx/bossmock.py (cut-out in the sorted layer + 10 hero copies, real hero invisible as the camera anchor): 400 world px wide ≈ 5.5× a hero, arena zoom PC ×0.8 / phone ×0.7 + camera pulled toward the boss (the phone skill wheel covers the right side). Flip changed to a SIDE topple (belly + heart face the players; the totem stump makes a full upside-down flip look wrong); stomp = front pair or hind pair. Animation = 11 image-to-video clips, prompts + table in doc §18 (cut with tools/vidclips.py). WEEK SET (owner 2026-11-22 21:00 'ขอทำ world boss ให้ครบทุกตัวก่อน เอาอีก6ตัวให้ครบ7วัน ขอพร้อมเอาไปดีไซน์หน่อย'): WORLD_BOSS_WEEK_TH.md = one world boss per weekday in the Thai day colours, each carrying one great shard of the Great Totem — Sun red Khepra the sun scarab (rolls a sun; brace circles stop it → open window; hide in obelisk shadows at noon; fire gems heat the sun, ice cools it) · Mon yellow Puijantra the moon rabbit (pounds mochi: eat them before it re-absorbs them; full moon = focus, new moon = 20 clones = spread) · Tue pink Burumba the war-drum mammoth (ranged break 4 drums that summon ghost waves; a shield line of ≥ 3 + N/8 players stops its charge → open window; guard vs rage) · Wed green Mayawee the thousand-hue chameleon (invisible; break colour pods to paint it visible 15 s; stack charges + quick; its art prompt uses a MAGENTA background) · Thu orange Brontara the thunderbird (in the sky; wake 5 lightning-rod totems, 3 caught strikes fire back and ground it; volt feeds it, the totem gem's pillar catches a strike) · Fri light-blue Lumina the morning-star sky whale (swallows the players in its mouth cone into a stomach room with the shard = main HP; outside players empty its KO gauge to make it cough; leech / siphon) · Sat purple Urtok. - Target lock (owner 2026-11-22 'ทำระบบล็อคเป้าแบบ12หาง … สมูท สเถียร เปลี่ยนเป้าง่ายๆไม่ค้าง', 12 Tails screenshot): target_lock.js (window.TL, loaded right after mobile.js). One locked enemy TL.e that STAYS while the hero walks (a ground click / the joystick only stops the attack); released by Esc, the ✕ on the target frame, holding 🎯 0.45 s, walking > 900 px away or another map; when it dies the next enemy fighting the hero (else the nearest ≤ 340 px on screen) is locked and the reticle glides there (enemy objects are reused on respawn, so a dead lock is dropped at once). PC: left click = lock + attack (the whole drawn body is the hit box: TL.pick replaces enemyAt; the current lock wins ties), right click = lock only, T / Shift+T = next / previous (e.code, works on a Thai layout). Phone: 🎯 tap = next target (lock only, the hero does not run off), hold = release; tapping a monster = lock + attack; attack button, skills, aim preview, drag-aim and ground taps use the lock. T / 🎯 walk a frozen list (bosses first, then aggressive, then distance, on screen ≤ 640 px) for 2.6 s, so repeated presses visit every enemy instead of bouncing between the two nearest. Skills use the lock when it is within the skill's range + 360 px (TL.forSkill / TL.near), else the nearest as before and the lock follows. Auto mode prefers the lock (TL.autoPrime / autoPick). Chase watchdog on the game clock: chasing (attack or walk-up-to-cast) without moving for 1.4 s or shuffling 3.5 s (net < 30 px, no closer) → the chase stops, the lock stays, message 'เข้าไม่ถึงเป้าหมาย — มีสิ่งกีดขวาง'. Look: red ring + 4 arrow darts on the body (gold darts + outer gold ring for bosses), drawn in screen space after mapOverlay (sharp at every zoom): intro overshoot, glide + 90° turn to a new target, tightens on hits, bursts on death, breathing, arrow at the screen edge when off screen, faint when the boss is hidden; thin ring at the feet + dashed hover ring (TL.ground replaces the old dashed rings). Target frame #tlf: portrait cropped from the monster's own idle sheet (head found from the alpha), level pill coloured by the level gap, Thai + English name, HP with a damage trail, break gauge, status chips, 'ดุ' / 'บอส' / 'หาย' marks, ✕; PC top centre, phone beside the second menu row. Hooks in index.html: enemyAt, render (TL.ground, TL.draw), useSkill, autoTick, overhead HP bar for TL.e. Tests in /home/claude/work/tlock: func.py [hero] [map] (PC mouse / keys), phone.py HERO W H, vis.py (animation sheet, TL.ts = slow motion); run one browser at a time — two in parallel slow the frames so clicks miss and the game-clock watchdog does not fire in time. PHONE v2 (owner 2026-11-23 'กดล็อกแล้วเลือดมอนขึ้นมาบังทุกอย่าง' + 'เลือกเป้าหมายได้ เหมือนเลือกพื้นที่ตอนใช้สกิล'): the phone frame is a slim chip (portrait 28 px, name row + 9 px HP bar, ✕ inside) that place() docks in a corner, never under the menus in the middle of the field: the top strip between the hero panel and the menu medallions when it is ≥ 160 px wide (class dkT, phones ≥ ~844 px), else right-aligned under the minimap (dkR; fades to .22 while the ✕ cancel spot shows = body.mobCx set by mobile.js), world boss under the panel (dkL); pets.js shows the tame button there as a small '🐾 ♡♡♡' pill under the chip. DRAG from 🎯 = pick the target yourself like a ground skill (mobile.js → TL.aimDrag(dx,dy,{T,room,cancel}) / TL.aimEnd(ok) / TL.aiming / TL.aimInfo): a blue ground circle (AIM_R 85) moves out from the hero, a full drag (64×sc px, less where the finger is near the screen edge) reaches the screen edge that way; candidate = the on-screen monster whose feet are nearest the circle (≤ 1.5 radii, the current one wins ties) → blue darts + 'Lv.n name' label (under the feet when the top strip would hide it, beside the skill wheel when the wheel would); lift = lock only, lift on ✕ = cancel (the circle freezes red, the old lock stays); tap = next target as before; hold still 0.45 s = release ARMED (button greys, buzz), done on lift. Tests (not in the repo) /home/claude/work/tlock2: drag.py TAG W H (picks / cancel / hold), show.py TAG W H (showcase frames), ph.py, pc.py (PC frame unchanged).

FOUND (all FIXED the same evening, owner 'แก้บัค 1 2 3 4' → next note): two-way heroes wolf / hippo / dragon / lion — branch-A 'x' skills are 70 % MATK (MIX), so STR builds bench 33–44 % under INT even with SP kept full; pure-ATK anubis / cat — INT builds match or beat STR because STR builds starve on SP (226 SP, +6 per 8 s vs INT 396, +23; anubis with SP kept full: STR 4,532 · INT 3,068); owl DEX ≈ 4,050 and jaguar B STR / AGI ≈ 3,500 = 1.7–2× the target (basic shots / swings are ATK only; tuned with the INT preset only). Proposed: A = ATK / B = MATK for two-way heroes (or 'x' = the higher), cheaper SP for physical skills or base SP regen, bench every branch at its best build. Bench artifact: hippo B (HIP.full) and jaguar B keep a gauge across bench runs in one page (hippo B INT 1,974 → 2,363 over 4 reps) — balanceBench's reset does not clear them; compare numbers on fresh pages.

- New-player guide (owner 2026-11-22 19:27 '3 แล้วก็ระบบสอนผู้เล่นในระบบต่างๆให้ใช้เป็น'): guide.js (loaded last, after testroom.js) + GUIDE_SYSTEM_TH.md (quest table, tutorials, how to add). Three parts: (1) เส้นทางนักผจญภัย = 24 quests in 5 chapters (Lv 1 → ~30), each teaching one system (stats, hunting, lock + AUTO, equip a gifted helm, sit, world map, first skill (gate = first level with a learnable skill AND a skill point: wolf 5, others 2), cast, refine, socket, shop, Lv 10, totem extract / place / gem cast, capital, market, storage, Lv 15, carve hole 2, Lv 25, map boss, seals, end); tracker card #gdQ (PC right under the minimap, phone compact left under the portrait — tap the title to expand, folds back after 9 s); progress from counters kept by hooks (onKill, castSkillNow, TL.set, mapRouteTo, polled AUTO / sitting / window opens / map visits) or from live state (pointsLeft, spUsed, EQ, weapon ref, orbs, totemGems.data() sx / ss / sm / cv, seals); small rewards that set up the next quest (helm → equip, refine stones → refine, orb → socket). (2) Coach marks (TUT, #gdC z 9000, non-blocking): dim + hole + pulsing ring + hand + bubble, PC / phone text and targets (#mgrid [data-m], #hotbar .hbt[data-k] / #mobSkW .mobSk[data-k], #mobJoy #mobAtk #mobTgt #mobAuto #mobSit #mobTab, #skw .tc.nx, #smw [data-tab], #rfw, #tgRight [data-tab], #tgSkills button, #tgUp, #tgBoard .tgN.ss / .smut, #mkx [data-mt] / [data-cat]); wait() advances by itself when the player does it; the first two quests open theirs once. (3) Book #gdBook (J or the new 📖 menu button 'mgguide'): quest log + 18 systems with ▶ สอน + เปิด + a switch for the tracker (test mode: tracker off by default, ↺ restart). 🧭 auto-path: BFS over VK_MAPS warps, mapRouteTo to the next warp each map, stops on a player move (click / WASD / joystick). Save af_guide_v1_<hero>_<mode>; old play saves at Lv 5+ skip the basic quests (⏭, no reward). ?guide=1 / 0. window.GUIDE (S, Q, TUT, SYS, runTut, jump, openBook, navStart …).
