- Emoji → painted icons (owner 2026-11-22 20:48 'อะไรที่ใช้อิโมจิ เจนเป็นภาพแทนได้ไหม'): ui_emoji.js (loaded right after assets/ui/emo/list.js, before assets.js) shows a picture for every emoji in UI text and canvas text: assets/ui/emo/<key>.webp when the key is in window.EMO_HAVE, else painted art already in the game (table T: glyph → key, fallback under assets/, 'md' = gold medallion cropped round when small), else the emoji stays. How: the Element.innerHTML setter + insertAdjacentHTML rewrite HTML before it is parsed (text outside tags only — never attributes, never inside select / textarea / script / style / svg; the getter returns the original string while the element is unchanged, so `if(el.innerHTML!==h)` code does not rewrite every frame), a MutationObserver wraps emoji in text nodes (textContent), CanvasRenderingContext2D fillText / strokeText draw the picture (NPC signs, DText; a lone icon ×1.55). Markup `<emo-i class="e-key"><emo-g>glyph</emo-g></emo-i>` (custom tags so no game span / i / b CSS hits them; the glyph stays inside, hidden → textContent and string comparisons keep working). 92 glyphs, ALL pictures since 2026-11-22 21:28 (the owner sent the 5 sheets of UI_ICON_PROMPTS_TH.md the same evening): 52 own icons in assets/ui/emo (sheet 1 buttons + guide, 2 totem + gems, 3 statuses + rewards + market, 4 green things on magenta, 5 the map boss statuses ✋ 🗝 🔇 🎵 🌑 👁 🕸 🦂; they also replaced the weak stand-ins of 💎 ⚗ 🌿 🔧 🔶 🔹 💬; 🗿 = stone idol head, also the 'turned to stone' status) + 40 that keep borrowed game art (menu / service / tab medallions, bag, coins, chest, scroll, book, potion, wing, pin, gem pictures); cut with `python3 tools/emo_icons.py cut <sheet> <png>` (writes the webp files, rebuilds list.js, bumps its ?v= in index.html; `board` = reference board for ChatGPT). Placeholder / title attributes cannot hold pictures (🔍 stays). ?emo=0 = off · window.EMO.st = cost counters (20 s of AUTO fighting ≈ 1.4 ms of script per second, buff bar rewritten once a second as before). New emoji in UI strings: add a row to T (and the key to a sheet in tools/emo_icons.py). A glyph with another meaning inside some windows goes in the ALT table of ui_emoji.js (glyph → [[CSS scope, key]], checked first for DOM text inside the scope, canvas always uses T, a scoped key without a picture keeps the emoji): 👁 = 'horus' (the Sphinx riddle's Eye of Horus, map_bosses_egy.js) everywhere, 'blind' only in #buffs. Sheet 6 IN (owner 21:36 'ได้เอาพร้อมมาเลย' → picture 21:54, cut with `python3 tools/emo_icons.py cut 6 <png>`): leaf 🍂 icecube 🧊 chains ⛓ (Viking boss move names) · sun ☀ moon 🌙 horus 👁 · sunrise 🌅 feet 🚶 cane 🦯 (riddle; 🐾 four legs = the sheet-4 paw) → 61 own icons, 100 glyphs in T, EMO_V 7; checked in game (/home/claude/work/emo/riddle6.py [fake=1|0]: riddle circles, question bubble, banners, cast bar, chat, buff bar). Not mapped yet (map_bosses_grk.js, added after sheet 6): 🐎 🪨 🐑 (move names) · 💫 🍷 (canvas, over dazed sheep / wine pools) — a sheet 7 if the owner wants them. Tests: /home/claude/work/emo (shots.py TAG hero w h emo, churn.py, cost.py, npc.py).
- New hero concepts (owner 2026-11-22 22:22 'ถ้าจะสร้างตัวละครใหม่ มีสกิลแยกร่างที่กดโจมตีจังหวะตามเรา เน้น auto spell ทั้งเวทย์และกายภาพ … เป็นสัตว์อะไรดี' → 22:25 'ขอพร้อมดีไซน์ทั้ง3ตัวหน่อย'): PROPOSAL + PROMPTS ONLY, nothing in the game — NEW_HERO_CONCEPTS_TH.md. Shared rules: clones copy the player's basic attacks and skills in rhythm (a share of the damage), auto spell = counters ('every nth hit'), two-way hero (A = ATK physical / B = MATK magic), mutation gems change what the clone copies; clone drawing already exists (KClone cat Shadow Dance, WClone wolf Valhalla). Lands: Norse / Aztec / Japan / Egypt 3 heroes each, Greek only 2. Candidates: (1, recommended) Gemini swan — Greek, fills the 3rd Greek slot, female duelist-dancer, black swan mirror twin from a water reflection, A = black swan blade clones / B = constellation auto-spells · (2) raven of Odin — Norse, Huginn (thought, rune magic) + Muninn (memory, replays strikes), eyepatch + wanderer hat + rune spear · (3) monkey king (Journey to the West hair clones, staff) — outside the 5 lands, needs a land decision. 3 design-sheet prompts (1536×1024 cream, big pose + turnaround + clone demo; attach the campfire key art + one existing hero sheet for proportions only). 22:33 'แต่ละตัว เพลสไตล์ต่างกันไหม' → the first draft gave all three the same formula (A every 4th hit, B every 3rd hit), so they were re-split: the clone differs by WHEN / WHERE / HOW MANY (doc table 'เพลย์สไตล์'): swan = ONE solid twin always on the far side of the target, same frame, swap places to dodge (single-target duelist, medium; A pincer counter → twin-blade auto skill, B stars left on the ground link into constellation lines = zone control) · raven = Muninn echoes us one beat late + Huginn flies off as a second cast point (planner, hard, mid-range spear; A stores up to 3 strikes and replays them at once into a boss open window, B every spell also comes out of Huginn) · monkey = swarm of weak hair clones that copy us and draw aggro (AoE farmer, easy / AUTO; A staff grows / shrinks, B cloud spirits + golden eyes that only HELP find a boss's real body, never skip the mechanic). Overlaps noted: cat Shadow Dance (C buff, 2 shoulder clones), owl (Norse bird, ranged), Bastet B (army). 22:40 'ขอดีไซน์อีกสหลายไฟสไตล์หน่อย' (= more styles) → 6 more (doc 'อีก 6 เพลย์สไตล์', each with its own auto-spell trigger): #4 hummingbird of Huitzilopochtli (Aztec; afterimages left where you dash, ≤ 3, triangle round the target = auto; hard; green feathers → turquoise / ruby design + magenta clip background; Greek alt = Ceryneian golden hind) · #5 kamaitachi sickle weasels (Japan; 2 brothers relay trip → cut → medicine, 3 steps on one target = auto; sustain skirmisher) · #6 Arachne (Greek; silk doll on a thread, the thread cuts / binds; A taut line every 4th hit, B cocoons turn monsters into puppets that copy our attacks, bosses only slowed) · #7 Huehuecoyotl coyote (Aztec; dancers ≤ 4 join on-beat attacks, a 4-beat bar = auto; AUTO gets 2 dancers — owner to decide) · #8 Bennu heron (Egypt; 2 fire doubles detonate, reborn from ash after 3 nearby hits, each rebirth = auto) · #9 Bödvar Bjarki's spirit bear / fylgja (Norse; bear fights beside and takes hits, 4 absorbed = counter; trance = body kneels, giant bear follows our buttons, body hit enough → spirit snaps back; easy). Monkey can move to the Aztec land (Legend of the Suns: people of the 2nd, wind sun became monkeys). Prompts for #4–#9 only after the owner picks. 22:57 (owner sent the raven v1 sheet he generated from prompt 2): 'ไม่ใช่ครับ ผมขอให้ออกแบบดีไซน์รูปลักษณ์อีกาเพิ่ม … มันไม่เท่ ส่วนคอนเซปต์สกิลผมชอบแล้ว' → the 22:40 message meant more LOOKS for the raven (misread; #4–#9 stay in the doc as spare ideas for a later hero). RAVEN CHOSEN, its skill concept approved (Muninn replays one beat late / stores 3 strikes · Huginn = second cast point, runes). Doc section 'อีกา: ลุคใหม่ 4 แบบ (22:57)': why v1 failed (wide hat hides the head and beak and reads as a wizard; fur collar + cloak + feather skirt = round blob; all navy / violet, blends with its own violet double; sleepy eye; dangling rune stones) → prompt 0 = rules sent once per ChatGPT chat (attach campfire art + the hero sheet the owner finds coolest, NOT the v1 raven): spiky swept-back crest, heavy hooked glossy beak, shaggy throat hackles, head / beak / one eye always visible, no hat / big hood / high collar, narrowed eyes, slim angular body, accents away from the doubles' frost blue / violet, few dangling items, no green; same sheet layout (big pose · turnaround · hero + 2 doubles) → 4 looks: 1 Sky Lancer (open silver wing-crest helm, dark-steel plate, blade-tipped folded wings, crimson scarf, Gungnir lance; diving strike) · 2 Shadow Hunter (short feather-edged hood, scar over the left eye, ash-white scarf, charcoal leather, crescent-hooked spear; crouch) · 3 One-Eyed Rune King (feather crown + gold circlet, ice-blue rune-crystal eye, feather mantle lined burgundy, 3 orbiting rune stones; spear to the sky) · 4 Twin Soul (right half Huginn silver / frost-blue eye, left half Muninn black feather half-cloak / violet smoke / gold eye, two-coloured blade) + a mix prompt + 'Make the hero female' tip. Recommended: 4 (ties to the skills) / 1 (safest cool). Waiting for his sheets; next = pick a look → 8-direction sheet on green → skill tree → 20 motion clips → icons / tree art / FX.

Taming: lock a 🐾 monster → '🐾 ผูกใจ' pill appended to the target frame #tlf (PC + phone) throws a charm of its land (capital monsters: any land) and tier ≥ the species' (Lv ≤ 18 tier 1, ≤ 36 tier 2), range 320, cd .45 s (game time) → the monster calms 12 s (stands still via the jgEnemyTick hook, re-wrapped by the keeper if a hero module replaces it; e.mbNoAuto; TL.autoPick / TL.autoPrime / command refuse it; player.attackTarget cleared every frame) → hearts 3 / 4 / 5 (critter / normal / swift-brute) → egg in the bag (e.dead + deadT 3.18 = respawns, no onKill). Any hurt() on it = hearts break + it gets angry; 12 s without a charm = timeout. Test mode: free throws, free hatching, codex 🧪 = pet at once, 🧪 bond +1. Hatch fee 500 / 1,500z, quality rolled with the gem odds + own pity 20 / 50 per hero, personality lines 1/1/2/2/3. Bonuses (bonusOf → BON in a gearCalc wrapper): main stat floor(bond/2) (+0/0/1/1/2 by quality from bond 2) · sub line (ATK / MATK 12, HIT / FLEE 10, DEF / MDEF 8, CRIT 4, perfect dodge 3 at bond 10, 30 % at bond 3) × quality ×1.00–1.40 · bond-10 gift stat +2 · personality lines; OFF when window.__RFL_OFF (the bench sets it) or __PET_OFF → standard bench unchanged (checked). Bond 0–1,000 (thresholds 0/40/100/180/280/400/540/700/850/1000): kill with Lv ≥ hero − 10 +1 (60 / day), boss +15, petting = click / tap the pet (capture pointerdown on the canvas) +2 every 10 min, cat sitting +1 / 2 min (20 / day). World pet: PetFx (sorted layer, MSPR_DRAW with a fake entity {spr, boss:1, bsc}, follows the hero's trail 58 behind and 26 to the side, idle behind the hero's facing, teleports when stuck / > 520, name tag + ♥ level under the feet hidden when right behind the hero, emotes heart / note / Zzz, rare+ glints) re-added by PetKeeper (layer 'none') after enterMap. - Monkey King (Sun Wukong) skill DESIGN (owner 2026-11-23 00:52 'ออโต้สเปลคือตีธรรมดาแล้วสกิลออก จุดเด่นคือ อัปแต่ตีไว เน้น cast skill autospell' · 00:53 'ลองออกแบบสกิลของหงอคง' · 00:53 'หงอคงขอเป็นกายภาพมีร่างแยกด้วยนะ'): PROPOSAL ONLY, nothing in the game — WUKONG_SKILLS_TH.md. Pure physical hero (A and B both ATK, branch scales like ANU_SCALE), staff, CHAR_ASPD 170 proposed. Core = autospell by counters: a landed normal swing = 1 beat (miss 0, crit 2 with Fiery Golden Eyes); each autospell skill counts its own beats and fires at N, performed by a golden HAIR CLONE (the hero's own frames, like the raven's Muninn) so the hero never stops swinging → ASPD is the stat to build; autospells cost no SP, never miss, have no cooldown; autospells / actives make no beats. A 'Havoc in Heaven' = one target, Rampage 0–10 (+1 ASPD each, flat), momentary clones strike from BEHIND the target (Ruyi Rhythm 3 · Thousand Staves 6 · Sky-Pillar Drop 10, every 5th A autospell giant ×2), Ruyi Shift short / long stance. B 'Seventy-Two Transformations' = persistent swarm 3 → 6 clones that copy swings, taunt (Bastet bTaunt pattern) and add +1 beat per hero swing; forms tiger / hornet / stone; Pile-On 6 · Staff Whirlwind 9 · Thousand-Monkey Barrage 15; Hair Recall cashes clones into beats. C 'Victorious Fighting Buddha' 12 nodes. Gems: autospells may sit in skill holes; a gem that changes cooldown changes N; 'stack' = the autospell stops firing by itself and stores 2 for a manual release. Keys gn / ga / gb / gx (wb* clashes with the world-boss arena props). LAND DECIDED 05:24 ('เป็นตัวหลุดมาจากรอยแยก'): a visitor who slipped out of the Aether Rift (the capital's sealed rift garden with cpRiftPortal + dungeons rift1–3) — create screen land 'รอยแยก', home capT, spawns by the capital's 'rift' warp; the 5-land promotion line stays, 'rift visitor' = the slot for future heroes outside the 5 myths. Same message 'สกิลมันจะโกงไปไหม' → doc section 'กันโกง': total damage is still tuned by the bench (≈ 2,050 per branch); the risks are SILENT ones the bench does not see, all fixed in the tables: skill ASPD total ≤ +10 (Headband +2, Rampage +1 per 2 stacks = +5, Shift ±3, no cap raise in the ULT) · per-autospell minimum gap N × 0.28 s (B N × 0.14 s), fixed, not changed by gems, binding at ASPD ≈ 186 (model: ASPD 184 → 190 damage ×1.60 without it vs ×1.33 with it; owl ×1.30) · counters keep leftover beats (max 2N) so gem cooldown multipliers scale N exactly · B clone copy-hits carry o._clone and never trigger on-hit gear / seals / runes / gem supports / pets / boss hit counters (Urtok legs) · clone regrowth ≥ 1.5 s, popped clones give no beat, ≤ 4 hits absorbed, bosses + 'ดุ' elites ignore the taunt · per-clone damage / ATK / DEF-cut count ≤ 6 clones (extra = wind clones), DEF cut ≤ −20, Hair Recall ≤ +6 beats · T4 basic = +1 beat per 3 (was N −1) · Golden Eyes cross out ONE fake per boss mechanic round. Autospell engine: a landed normal swing = 1 beat (crit 2 with gnEyes, 3 in the Furnace, ×2 for 3 s after a Somersault Cloud landing; B: ≥ 2 clones landing a copy = +1, gxCount 2, ult +1); each autospell (AUTOS gbBarrage gaPillar gbWhirl gaThousand gbPile gnHair gaRhythm, big first, 0.1 s apart) counts its own beats (cap 2N) and fires at N once its minimum gap N × 0.2 s (Basic / A) · N × 0.1 s (B) has passed (lowered from the design's 0.28 / 0.14: with cloud / crits / assist every build hit the old gaps and STR beat AGI); gem heavy / rapid → N ×1.5 / ×0.6 (gemNMul). Clones = the hero's own frames (gradedFrame FLT.*, ×CHAR_SCALE): WkMom momentary (A autospells, giant spirit for Sky-Pillar / Treasure) · WkMonkey persistent swarm (B; forms tiger / hornet / stone, wind clones, tmp clones for Barrage / Storm). One hair supply: while the swarm is out the A tools rest (aRest: Rampage gain + A autospells), B autospells need the swarm. Rampage WK.rp (cap 6/8/10 +2 gxHavoc, −2/s after 2.5 s, ASPD +1 per 2 ≤ +5, locked full during the ult / Equal to Heaven). Skill ASPD (skillAspd ≤ +10) → jgAspd converts RO points into the swing multiplier, cap SYS.aspdMax (+3 with the AGI tier). A normal swing lasts one attack interval: gStrike I = ACTION_TEMPO / (aspd() · 1.45). Copy-hits = o.dot hurt() (gCopy: seals / runes / gem supports / item procs / boss hit counters skip them), their numbers merged per target every 0.35 s (dmgNum wrapper). Decoys in jgPreHit (non-boss monsters within 60 / stone 100 of a non-wind clone; a clone takes one blow per 0.8 s; hits 2/3 + VIT/40 ≤ 4, hornet 1), then Hair Substitute. Other hooks: jgDmgMul (Rebel King per point + Golden Fur per clone ≤ 6, flat ≤ the hit's own power; Clinging Fur o._defCut ≤ 20), jgAbsorb (Forge Lv × n, ×2 in the Furnace; Cave), jgAfterHurt (crit of o.auto → beats), gearCalc (Eyes HIT), playerCC (Headband: shorter stun / flinch); the Eyes cross out one Huldra leaf mirage / Jonin room fake per round (e._wkFake). WkCore (layer 'none', gEnsure / window.gEnsure) runs the autospells, Rampage decay, decoys, Mob / Cave slows, ult ends (equalToHeaven, armyEnds + Handsome), storm ticks, Forge burns −2 s, status icons wkRp / wkSw / wkForm / wkShift, cdSync (level-based cooldowns of gnCloud / gnSub / gaUlt / gbUlt, skipped when a gem changed them) and the autospell bar #wkBar (one ring per learned autospell = beats / N, gold flash on fire, darker during the gap; above the hot bar / phone wheel, z 12 under the windows). Forms (zdTransform / zdRevert): A Magma Drake ×1.35 shield lv × 6 (+VIT × 2 Lava Plate), B Mountain Drake ×1.6 shield lv × 8, move −40 (jgMove); 0.8 s invulnerable; drain 5/s (Heir 4); max [16,19,22,25] s + 8 (Heir / Prophecy); no monster CC (jgNoInterrupt + 'hurt' actions dropped in a startAction wrapper); revert on empty / time / sitting / ult finisher / Heart pressed again — a press WITH a target (AUTO, bench) never reverts. Same buttons: zdButton(key) → ZD_DRAGON[key] in the matching form (other branch: refused + grey on the hot bar / wheel, .zdHo overlay; own branch: dragon badge); castSkillNow wrapper shows ZD_DN names. Damage: hurt() × ROLES.dmgK(o) (role k × CAL; the branch of the hit = its skill > o.br > normal attack > the skill being cast; Basic branch + normal attacks = the BUILD role = branch with more skill levels, test mode / tie = stronger power for two-way heroes, A for pure ones, window.__ROLE_BR overrides — every bench script sets it); skTip numbers × ROLES.kOfSkill. CC: SK_CC rewritten at load by a budget per role (SK_CC0 = originals; controller flinch → real stun, debuffer → slow, damage / mage / support / summoner keep 1 interrupt per branch, fighter / tank 2 + counters, Basic branch 1); ccGate in hurt = live rule for o.cc / normal attacks + monster CC resistance (hard CC → immune its length + 3 s 'ต้านทาน', flinch + .7 s); hero code that sets statuses itself is untouched. KO drain × role (window.skKO wrapped). Max HP: role HP per level added to the base in derive() (× (1+VIT/100)); tank build: monster hits −Lv × .4 (ENH_ABSORB wrapper). 'dot' hits on one target within .28 s show one merged number (dmgNum wrapper; hurt passes _dot). Looks: role chips (custom tag <rl-c>, code SVG icons) on the skill window A / B tabs + a role line under each branch title (test mode: 'ทดสอบบทบาทนี้' button) and on the create screen.
